# Proposed OEIS comments: A233687 to A235280

One section per OEIS entry: the date the result was obtained, the paper that proves it, what the entry still records as unsettled, and the text proposed for posting on that entry.

**Nothing here has been posted.** See [../SUBMITTING.md](../SUBMITTING.md).

---

## A233687

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A233687>  
Result obtained: **31 August 2026**  
Paper: [5570](../papers/05501-06000/05570-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 4*a(n-1) +222*a(n-2) -895*a(n-3) -14806*a(n-4) +61290*a(n-5) +479263*a(n-6) -2067766*a(n-7) -8897249*a(n-8) +40799029*a(n-9) +102512949*a(n-10) -513857547*a(n-11) -760171900*a(n-12) +4350919810*a(n-13) +3628256006*a(n-14) -25577829290*a(n-15) -10454924632*a(n-16) +106480049675*a(n-17) +12586615989*a(n-18) -317308345201*a(n-19) +27167936641*a(n-20) +679221981450*a(n-21) -157201990004*a(n-22) -1040817140533*a(n-23) +351215509546*a(n-24) +1129423930543*a(n-25) -469396649218*a(n-26) -850891317254*a(n-27) +403441486513*a(n-28) +431368323585*a(n-29) -223830914820*a(n-30) -140453027219*a(n-31) +77851050483*a(n-32) +27476109266*a(n-33) -16042803856*a(n-34) -2950519700*a(n-35) +1797075893*a(n-36) +152255054*a(n-37) -93497323*a(n-38) -3429989*a(n-39) +1518688*a(n-40) +74280*a(n-41).

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 1 024 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 41. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A233712

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A233712>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [373](../papers/00001-00500/00373-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 75 (see link above)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 625 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 75, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

```

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical for column k:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrences in the linked file are correct. Column k counts arrays of a fixed width, so it is a walk count in a finite graph and satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence; Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one, its order is the order stated for that column, and so is the order of every tail, so any recurrence of that order is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A234259

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A234259>  
Result obtained: **31 August 2026**  
Paper: [10709](../papers/10501-11000/10709-PROOF.pdf)

> Empirical: a(n) = 2*a(n-1) + 6*a(n-2) - 12*a(n-3).
> Conjectures from _Colin Barker_, Oct 14 2018: (Start)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```

```

## A234269

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A234269>  
Result obtained: **25 August 2026**  
Paper: [11619](../papers/11501-12000/11619-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Conjecture D-finite with recurrence: -(n+1)*(2*n-5)*a(n) -2*n*(n-4)*a(n-1) +4*(n-1)*(2*n-3)*a(n-2) +2*(8*n^2-32*n+29)*a(n-3) +4*(n-2)*(2*n-7)*a(n-4)=0. - _R. J. Mathar_, Jan 24 2020

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
Not only empirical: the generating function given above is algebraic, and a recurrence with polynomial coefficients is a differential operator applied to it. Reducing that operator leaves a polynomial of degree 3, so the recurrence holds for n > 3. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

Paper: [453](../papers/00001-00500/00453-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 52 (see link above)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 343 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 52, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A234545

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A234545>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [462](../papers/00001-00500/00462-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 49 (see link above).

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 1296 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 49, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A235102

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 1512 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 62, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 2744 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one.
